# Sieve3 V2 Resolved Parameters For FreqAI

## 1. Scope

This is a compact extraction for a separate FreqAI stress-testing task while the remaining Sieve3 campaign continues elsewhere.

- Source runtime: `user_data/Custom_Launcher/launcher_v2/runtime/entry_sieve_v2_speed_recovery`.
- Completed active surface checked: `421` strategies.
- Positive validation rows found: `197`.
- Retained here: `189` positive rows at or below 10% validation drawdown across `63` locked entry sources.
- Excluded for big drawdown: `8` otherwise-profitable rows above 10% drawdown. Their raw evidence remains untouched.
- Also excluded: archived, isolated, deleted, superseded, failed, zero-trade, flat, and losing branches; temporary target-quality work; and the still-running final generic-level campaign.
- Sparse evidence: `24` retained rows have fewer than 10 validation trades. They are flagged, not silently rejected, because rare patterns can legitimately trade infrequently.

Here, **resolved** means the exact best parameter vector saved by that Hyperopt and used by its completed validation backtest. It does not mean production-promoted. Each row is a self-contained exit solution; different rows and exit families are not expected to converge on the same values.

## 2. Integrity Checks

- `189/189` selected parameter files exist.
- `189/189` selected sell-key sets exactly match each strategy's `ACTIVE_SELL_PARAMS`.
- `189/189` current `LOCKED_BUY_PARAMS` match the frozen runtime strategy that produced the result.
- Entry values omit unambiguous child settings belonging only to common disabled branches. A value is retained when it may be shared with another enabled path; every disabled branch name remains explicit.
- Exit values contain only the selected active sell parameters, not ranges, defaults, or inactive alternatives.
- Win rate and drawdown are converted from Freqtrade fractions to percentages. Profit is read from the already-percent {B}profit_total_pct{B} field.

## 3. Runtime Contract

| Setting | Value |
|---|---|
| Hyperopt space | `sell` |
| Sieve exit overlay | `control_entry_exits=false` |
| Loss | `MultiMetricHyperOptLoss` |
| Seed | `42` |
| Training | `20200101-20221231` |
| Validation | `20240401-20260401` |
| Pairs | BTC, ETH, SOL perpetuals |
| Wallet | 1000 USDT reference wallet |

## 5. Use In The FreqAI Task

1. Treat each row as a concrete entry-plus-exit hypothesis with fixed inputs, not as a broad Hyperopt space.
2. Use the locked entry values and selected exit values exactly as shown before adding FreqAI context.
3. Compare FreqAI overlays against the same fixed strategy row so any difference is attributable to the overlay.
4. Keep entry source, exit family, selected values, timerange, pairs, and result artifact attached to every derived dataset row.
5. Do not merge several exit rows merely because they share an entry. They are distinct solutions unless later evidence supports combining them.
6. Sparse rows need wider or pattern-appropriate validation before strong claims, but low trade count alone is not a defect for rare patterns.
7. Update this handover only from completed clean rows. The ongoing final generic-level campaign is intentionally absent from this snapshot.
